"""PR-2 (B2 upload + B3 download) live end-to-end against a running server."""
import os
import sqlite3
import sys
import time

import httpx

sys.path.insert(0, os.getcwd())
from backend.utils.hashing import compute_video_id  # noqa: E402

BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:8123"
SRC = "output/mahadevpura_smoke_180s.mp4"
VID = "05e0e577029daafcf425ca70c0c4f0a3"  # indexed smoke video (1 confirmed segment)
PART = 30 * 1024 * 1024

results = []


def check(name, cond, extra=""):
    results.append((name, bool(cond)))
    print(f"{'PASS' if cond else 'FAIL'}  {name} {extra}")


with httpx.Client(timeout=120.0) as c:
    # readiness
    for _ in range(40):
        try:
            if c.get(BASE + "/api/config").status_code == 200:
                break
        except httpx.TransportError:
            time.sleep(0.5)
    else:
        sys.exit("server never came up")

    # --- B2 negatives ---
    r = c.post(BASE + "/api/upload/init", json={"filename": "evil.exe"})
    check("init rejects bad extension (400)", r.status_code == 400, r.text[:60])
    r = c.post(BASE + "/api/upload/init", json={"filename": "../../escape.mp4"})
    check("init rejects traversal (400)", r.status_code == 400)
    r = c.put(BASE + "/api/upload/nope/part/0", content=b"x")
    check("part rejects unknown id (404)", r.status_code == 404)

    # --- B2 happy path: 3-part chunked upload, byte identity ---
    src_md5 = compute_video_id(SRC)
    size = os.path.getsize(SRC)
    r = c.post(BASE + "/api/upload/init",
               json={"filename": "e2e_upload.mp4", "total_size_bytes": size})
    check("init ok", r.status_code == 200, r.text[:80])
    uid = r.json()["upload_id"]

    r = c.put(f"{BASE}/api/upload/{uid}/part/1", content=b"x")
    check("out-of-order part rejected (409)", r.status_code == 409)

    n = 0
    with open(SRC, "rb") as fh:
        while True:
            blob = fh.read(PART)
            if not blob:
                break
            r = c.put(f"{BASE}/api/upload/{uid}/part/{n}", content=blob)
            assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
            n += 1
    print(f"      uploaded {n} parts, {size} bytes")

    r = c.post(f"{BASE}/api/upload/{uid}/complete", json={"total_parts": n})
    check("complete ok", r.status_code == 200, r.text[:120])
    body = r.json()
    check("assembled MD5 == source MD5 (byte identity)", body["video_id"] == src_md5,
          f"{body['video_id'][:12]} vs {src_md5[:12]}")
    check("assembled size matches", body["size_bytes"] == size)
    up_path = body["path"]
    check("file landed under upload_dir", os.path.isfile(up_path), up_path)

    # --- B3: compile the real confirmed segment, then download it ---
    db = sqlite3.connect("output/sports_indexer.db")
    seg_id = db.execute("SELECT id FROM play_segments WHERE video_id=?", (VID,)).fetchone()[0]
    r = c.post(BASE + "/api/compile",
               json={"video_id": VID, "segment_ids": [seg_id],
                     "output_filename": "e2e_reel.mp4"})
    check("compile ok", r.status_code == 200, r.text[:120])

    r = c.get(f"{BASE}/api/highlights/{VID}/e2e_reel.mp4")
    check("download streams the reel (200)", r.status_code == 200)
    check("reel has bytes", len(r.content) > 100_000, f"{len(r.content)} bytes")
    check("content-type video", "video" in r.headers.get("content-type", ""))

    r = c.get(f"{BASE}/api/highlights/{VID}/..%2F..%2Fconfig.json")
    check("download rejects traversal (400)", r.status_code == 400, str(r.status_code))
    r = c.get(f"{BASE}/api/highlights/{'0' * 32}/e2e_reel.mp4")
    check("download rejects unknown video (404)", r.status_code == 404)

    # cleanup the uploaded copy (keep the reel for inspection)
    os.remove(up_path)

failed = [n for n, ok in results if not ok]
print(f"\n{'=' * 50}\nE2E: {len(results) - len(failed)}/{len(results)} passed")
sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)

Done — both PRs merged with the full diligence chain. Master is at `020ff61`, zero open PRs.

**What merged:**
- [#99 — chunked upload + highlight download (B2+B3, PR-2)](https://github.com/avidullu/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/99)
- [#100 — docs status sync](https://github.com/avidullu/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/100)

**Due diligence performed, in order:**

1. **Tests** — full suite 388 green locally (including the 15 new upload/download tests) plus all four CI lanes green on both PRs.
2. **Live E2E for the risky change** (the new HTTP surface) — real server on `:8123`, real artifacts:
   - **Upload:** the 86MB smoke video sent in 3 sequential parts, reassembled to **MD5 byte-identity** with the source (the returned `video_id` matched `compute_video_id` of the original exactly).
   - **Negative paths:** out-of-order part → 409, bad extension and traversal filenames → 400, unknown upload id → 404.
   - **Download hardening, both layers:** backslash-encoded traversal reaches the handler and gets the safe-name 400; `%2F`-encoded traversal never even reaches it (router 404). The one e2e "failure" was my test expecting 400 where the router answers first — defense verified intact at both layers.
   - **The actual CUJ tail:** compiled the real confirmed rally from this morning's smoke run into a 3.9MB reel and streamed it back through `GET /api/highlights/...` — so the `segment_ids` compile fix and the download link are proven against real data, not just mocks.
3. **Doc updates** (#100) — `NEXT_STEPS.md` header now shows PR-2 merged plus a summary of the #90–#98 quality sweep; `UI_ALPHA_EXECUTION_PLAN.md` has PR-2 checked off with the E2E evidence inline; and `BACKLOG.md`'s shlex-quote WSL item is formally closed by #97 (with the residual tenancy-era note kept honest).
4. **Memory** — current-state checkpoint updated to the merged reality.

Your tomorrow-morning CUJ runbook is now fully unblocked: `python -m backend.main --server` → paste path → index → select rallies → compile → the ⬇ download link that, as of today, actually works. The one thing to remember when PR-3 (identity) lands: `security.allowed_video_root` must contain `upload_dir`, or `/api/process` will reject uploaded paths — it's flagged in the plan.
